---
title: "PRIN 2026: A First Look at Ranked Projects and Funding"
description: "A descriptive view of 15 PRIN 2026 sectors: final rankings, fundable projects, institutional participation, and MUR allocations, with interactive tables and charts."
date: 2026-10-07 12:00:00 +0200
tags: [Research Funding, PRIN 2026, Research Perspectives]
published: true
---

**Coverage:** 15 PRIN 2026 sectors, as published on 7 October 2026. This is partial data and not a ranking of institutions. It will be updated as more sectors are published.

The published PRIN 2026 documents offer an opportunity to look carefully at
how research funding is distributed. Across the **15 sectors examined here**,
there are **205 projects in the final rankings**, **124 projects identified as
fundable**, and **€133,232,298 in MUR allocations**.

These figures describe the documents collected on 7 October 2026. They cover
part of the call. I hope this overview offers a useful starting point for
reading the results, while leaving the interpretation close to the evidence.

> **How the data were processed:** Figures were extracted from the official MUR PDFs with AI assistance, then checked: the extracted allocations match each sector's printed "TOTALE COMPLESSIVO" exactly. Treat them as indicative and verify against the official documents before relying on them. Corrections are welcome at [gstilo@luiss.it](mailto:gstilo@luiss.it).

## Methodology

The analysis used **46 PDFs**, including paired final rankings and funding
tables for **15 sectors**. Each sector has a **Table A**, containing the final
ranking, and a **Table B**, listing the projects identified for funding and
their research-unit allocations. Here, “final-ranked” means appearing in
Table A, and “fundable” means appearing in Table B. The reported euros are
allocations, rather than verified payments.

Projects were matched using their application codes; institutions were grouped
using their **11-digit tax codes**. Funding was summed from the final
**“Contributo MUR assegnato”** column for each research unit, excluding
cofunding, total project costs, and repeated project subtotals.

The extracted allocations reconcile exactly with every covered sector’s
printed **“TOTALE COMPLESSIVO”**. Each fundable project is present in its
sector’s final ranking and has one PI unit. For PE7, the correction issued in
**DDG 45 replaces DDG 32**; its tables are counted once. These checks support
the arithmetic; the figures describe the supplied documents, with their
coverage and missing information kept visible.

Of the 205 final-ranked projects, **60.5% appear in Table B**. The remaining
**81** are in the final ranking but outside its funding list. The PDFs do not
give the number of all submitted proposals, so 60.5% should be read as a share
of the final ranking. It does not establish an applicant success rate.

## Read the sectors within this coverage

LS7 has the largest final ranking in this set: **28 projects**, of which
**17 are fundable**, with **€17.88 million allocated**. PE8 follows with
**22 final-ranked projects**, **14 fundable**, and **€16.46 million**.
Together, their allocations account for **25.8%** of the covered total.

The fundable share of the final ranking ranges from **50.0%** in PE2, PE9,
and SH4 to **75.0%** in LS4. Those percentages sit alongside different project
counts: LS4 has 12 fundable projects among 16 final-ranked proposals, while
PE9 has 4 among 8. Keeping the counts visible helps give the percentages scale.

The covered sectors can also be grouped into their macrosectors:

| Covered macrosector | Final-ranked | Fundable | Fundable / ranked | MUR allocated |
|:--|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| Life Sciences (LS) | 59 | 38 | 64.4% | €40,069,402 |
| Physical Sciences and Engineering (PE) | 100 | 60 | 60.0% | €65,581,509 |
| Social Sciences and Humanities (SH) | 46 | 26 | 56.5% | €27,581,387 |

The selection includes four LS, eight PE, and three SH sectors. The amounts
therefore describe different portions of each macrosector. They cannot establish
a comparison of the complete macrosector budgets.

## Institutional participation and project leadership

The 124 fundable projects involve **81 institutions** and **621 research
units**: an average of **5.01 units per project**. A single project can involve
several institutions. Each institution is counted once per project in which it
participates, and receives only its own research units’ allocations in the
financial totals below.

The **Consiglio Nazionale delle Ricerche** has the largest allocation in this
set, **€10,848,262**, across **44 projects**, including **8 led as PI**.
**Sapienza University of Rome** participates in **47 projects**, including
**7 led as PI**, with **€9,565,134 allocated**. The two figures illustrate how
participation counts and funding amounts can place institutions in a different
order.

For leadership, **Padova and Milano each lead 9 projects** in the covered
funding lists. Across all 81 institutions, leadership counts sum to 124: each
project has one PI. Participation counts count each institution involved in a
consortium, so they add up to more than the number of distinct projects.

The five institutions with the largest allocations together receive
**€38,029,677**, or **28.5%** of the covered funds. This describes the allocation
pattern in this set; assessing its causes would require further information,
including submission volumes, institutional size, and the research undertaken.

</section>

## Collaboration through shared projects

The same funding tables also describe who participates together. Each institution
is a node; two institutions have an undirected tie when they appear in at least
one common fundable project. A consortium contributes every pair of its distinct
institutions. Multiple research units from one institution count as one member
of that project.

For any selected view, the tie weight is:

**shared fundable projects ÷ all fundable projects in that view**.

The denominator is **124** for the overall graph, or the sector’s number of
fundable projects for a sector graph. A weight of 1 means the pair participates
together in every fundable project in that view; a pair with no shared projects
has weight 0 and no edge. Filtering weaker ties keeps this denominator fixed.
The scale expresses a share of the view’s projects, not a funding share or a
probability of future collaboration.

Across the 15 covered sectors, the overall graph has **81 institutions** and
**762 distinct collaborating pairs**. It forms **one connected component**:
institutions can be connected through chains of shared projects. The funding
records contain **1,285 pair–project occurrences**; recurring partnerships
contribute several occurrences to the same tie.

The strongest overall tie is **CNR–Sapienza**, with **16 shared projects**.
Its weight is **16 / 124 = 0.1290**, or **12.9%**. Sector graphs use their own
denominators: the same number of shared projects can represent a larger share
within a smaller sector view.

These graphs describe joint membership in the funded consortia. Larger consortia
create more pairwise links: a project with five institutions contributes ten
pairs. The data do not measure how much work each pair performs together, and
layout proximity serves as a visual aid rather than another statistic.

### Explore the collaboration networks

Choose **Overall** or a sector. Drag institutions, pan the canvas, and zoom to
inspect labels. Select a node or tie for its project codes; search can focus an
institution and its visible partners. **Rearrange** runs an animated force-directed
layout, and **Stop movement** holds the positions for inspection. The minimum
shared-project filter simplifies the view while retaining all its institutions.

</section>

## What these figures leave open

The final rankings identify PIs but do not provide institutional affiliations
for unfunded projects. The supplied PDFs therefore do not support submission
counts, eligibility totals, or applicant success rates by institution. These
fields are marked **N/A** in the workbook. An institution absent from the
funding lists may still have submitted proposals.

Differences between sectors or institutions invite further questions, but the
data here do not explain them. A fuller account would need the complete
submission and eligibility records, coverage of the remaining sectors, and
context about project costs and institutional participation.

## Sources and data

The official starting point is the
[PRIN 2026 ranking publication page](https://prin.mur.gov.it/Pages/Index/301).
The sector pages below provide the decrees and their attached Tables A and B
used in this analysis. These links follow the source pages recorded when the
PDFs were collected on 7 October 2026.

| Covered macrosector | Official sector pages |
|:--|:--|
| Life Sciences (LS) | [LS2](https://www.mur.gov.it/it/atti-e-normativa/bando-prin-2026-ddg-6-ottobre-2026-n-37-macrosettore-ls-settore-ls2-approvazione) · [LS3](https://www.mur.gov.it/it/atti-e-normativa/bando-prin-2026-ddg-6-ottobre-2026-n-38-macrosettore-ls-settore-ls3-approvazione) · [LS4](https://www.mur.gov.it/it/atti-e-normativa/bando-prin-2026-ddg-6-ottobre-2026-n-42-macrosettore-ls-settore-ls4-approvazione) · [LS7](https://www.mur.gov.it/it/atti-e-normativa/bando-prin-2026-ddg-6-ottobre-2026-n-39-macrosettore-ls-settore-ls7-approvazione) |

For **PE7**, the linked page contains DDG 45 of 6 October 2026, which corrects
and replaces DDG 32. The PE7 tables are counted once.

- [Download the Excel summary]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-summary.xlsx' | relative_url }}), including definitions, PDF references, and research-unit audit data.
- Download CSV tables: [sectors]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-sectors.csv' | relative_url }}), [institutions]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-institutions.csv' | relative_url }}), and [institutions by sector]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-institution-sectors.csv' | relative_url }}).
- [Download the chart data as JSON]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-data.json' | relative_url }}).

- Collaboration downloads: [all 16 networks as JSON]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-collaboration.json' | relative_url }}), [pair weights (CSV)]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-network-edges.csv' | relative_url }}), [institutions and node numbers (CSV)]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-network-nodes.csv' | relative_url }}), and [network overview (CSV)]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-network-summary.csv' | relative_url }}).
- [GraphML files for all 16 views]({{ '/assets/data/prin-2026-graphml.zip' | relative_url }}) can be imported into network-analysis tools.
- The interactive graphs use [Cytoscape.js](https://js.cytoscape.org/) with its built-in CoSE force-directed layout. Version 3.33.1 and its MIT license are bundled with this post so the library can load locally.

3},{"tax":"80088230018","sector":"SH4","projects":2,"led":0,"units":2,"amount":485653},{"tax":"80088230018","sector":"SH5","projects":3,"led":0,"units":3,"amount":610512},{"tax":"80088230018","sector":"SH6","projects":3,"led":2,"units":3,"amount":838024},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"LS2","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":247098},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"LS4","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":158609},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"LS7","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":161510},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"PE1","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":166171},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"PE7","projects":2,"led":1,"units":2,"amount":407665},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"PE8","projects":2,"led":1,"units":2,"amount":409405},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"PE10","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":232505},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"SH4","projects":4,"led":2,"units":4,"amount":966637},{"tax":"00340520220","sector":"SH6","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":221389},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"LS3","projects":1,"led":1,"units":1,"amount":279792},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"LS4","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":194295},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"LS7","projects":1,"led":1,"units":1,"amount":247730},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"PE2","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":197714},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"PE3","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":172714},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"PE9","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":163557},{"tax":"80013890324","sector":"SH4","projects":1,"led":1,"units":1,"amount":212539},{"tax":"80014550307","sector":"PE8","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":241037},{"tax":"82002850418","sector":"PE10","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":182613},{"tax":"82002850418","sector":"SH6","projects":1,"led":0,"units":1,"amount":176970},{"tax":"93009870234","sector":"LS4","projects":1,"led":1,"units":1,"amount":324954},{"tax":"93009870234","sector":"LS7","projects":1,"led":0,"units <script>
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const data = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('prin-data').textContent);
  const eur = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-GB', { style:'currency', currency:'EUR', maximumFractionDigits:0 });
  const integer = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-GB');
  const percent = n => `${(n * 100).toFixed(1)}%`;
  const million = n => `€${(n / 1000000).toFixed(2)}m`;
  const state = { sectorKey:'sector', sectorDir:1, institutionKey:'amount', institutionDir:-1 };
  const sortRows = (rows, key, dir) => [...rows].sort((a,b) => {
    if (key === 'sector') {
      const macro = { LS:0, PE:1, SH:2 };
      return dir * (macro[a.sector.replace(/\d/g,'')] - macro[b.sector.replace(/\d/g,'')] || Number(a.sector.replace(/\D/g,'')) - Number(b.sector.replace(/\D/g,'')));
    }
    const order = typeof a[key] === 'string' ? a[key].localeCompare(b[key]) : a[key] - b[key];
    return dir * order || String(a.name || a.sector).localeCompare(String(b.name || b.sector));
  });
  function institutionsForScope(scope, search) {
    const partial = new Map(data.bySector.filter(r => r.sector === scope).map(r => [r.tax,r]));
    return data.institutions.map(r => scope === 'all' ? {...r} : (partial.has(r.tax) ? {...r,...partial.get(r.tax)} : null))
      .filter(r => r && r.name.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(search.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()));
  }
  function writeTable(id, rows, columns) {
    const body = document.querySelector(`#${id} tbody`);
    const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
      columns.forEach(([key,format]) => {
        const cell = document.createElement('td');
        cell.textContent = format ? format(row[key]) : String(row[key]);
        tr.appendChild(cell);
      });
      fragment.appendChild(tr);
    });
    if (!rows.length) {
      const tr = document.createElement('tr'); const td = document.createElement('td');
      td.colSpan = columns.length; td.textContent = 'No funded institutions match this search.'; tr.appendChild(td); fragment.appendChild(tr);
    }
    body.replaceChildren(fragment);
  }
  function updateSort(id,key,dir) {
    document.querySelectorAll(`#${id} th`).forEach(th => {
      if (th.dataset.key === key) th.setAttribute('aria-sort',dir === 1 ? 'ascending' : 'descending');
      else th.removeAttribute('aria-sort');
    });
  }
  function drawBars(id, rows, key, stacked=false) {
    const container = document.getElementById(id);
    const maximum = key === 'rate' ? 1 : Math.max(1,...rows.map(r => stacked ? r.ranked : r[key]));
    const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
    rows.forEach(r => {
      const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className='prin-bar-row';
      const label = document.createElement('span'); label.className='prin-bar-label';label.textContent=r.name || r.sector;label.title=label.textContent;
      const track = document.createElement('span');track.className='prin-track';track.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      const fill = document.createElement('span');fill.className='prin-bar-funded';fill.style.width=`${(stacked ? r.funded : r[key]) / maximum * 100}%`;track.appendChild(fill);
      if (stacked) {
        const unfunded = document.createElement('span');unfunded.className='prin-bar-unfunded';unfunded.style.width=`${r.unfunded / maximum * 100}%`;track.appendChild(unfunded);
      }
      const value = document.createElement('span');value.className='prin-bar-value';
      value.textContent=stacked ? `${r.funded} / ${r.ranked}` : key === 'amount' ? million(r.amount) : key === 'rate' ? percent(r.rate) : integer.format(r[key]);
      row.append(label,track,value);fragment.appendChild(row);
    });
    container.replaceChildren(fragment);
  }
  function renderSectors() {
    const rows = sortRows(data.sectors,state.sectorKey,state.sectorDir);
    writeTable('prin-sectors-table',rows,[['sector'],['ranked',integer.format],['funded',integer.format],['unfunded',integer.format],['rate',percent],['amount',eur.format]]);
    updateSort('prin-sectors-table',state.sectorKey,state.sectorDir);
    const metric=document.getElementById('prin-sector-metric').value;
    drawBars('prin-sector-chart',rows,metric === 'counts' ? 'funded' : metric,metric === 'counts');
    document.getElementById('prin-sector-legend').hidden=metric !== 'counts';
    document.getElementById('prin-sector-caption').textContent=metric === 'counts'
      ? 'PRIN 2026, fundable vs. final-ranked projects in 15 covered sectors (snapshot 7 Oct 2026). Green: fundable (Table B). Grey: final-ranked, not in Table B. Shares are of the final ranking, not applicant success rates.'
      : metric === 'amount' ? 'PRIN 2026, MUR allocations to fundable projects (Table B) in 15 covered sectors (partial data, snapshot 7 Oct 2026). Bars share a zero baseline; labels are rounded to millions of euros. Exact amounts are in the table. Allocations do not establish applicant success rates.'
      : 'PRIN 2026, fundable share of the final ranking in 15 covered sectors (partial data, snapshot 7 Oct 2026). Fundable (Table B) projects divided by final-ranked (Table A) projects; the scale runs from 0% to 100%. Shares are of the final ranking, not applicant success rates.';
  }
  function renderInstitutions() {
    const scope=document.getElementById('prin-institution-sector').value;
    const search=document.getElementById('prin-institution-search').value;
    const metric=document.getElementById('prin-institution-metric').value;
    const limit=Number(document.getElementById('prin-institution-limit').value);
    const filtered=institutionsForScope(scope,search);
    writeTable('prin-institutions-table',sortRows(filtered,state.institutionKey,state.institutionDir),[['name'],['projects',integer.format],['led',integer.format],['units',integer.format],['amount',eur.format]]);
    updateSort('prin-institutions-table',state.institutionKey,state.institutionDir);
    const top=sortRows(filtered,metric,-1).slice(0,limit);
    drawBars('prin-institution-chart',top,metric);
    const allInScope=institutionsForScope(scope,'');
    const sum=filtered.reduce((n,r) => n+r.amount,0);
    document.getElementById('prin-institution-status').textContent=`${scope === 'all' ? 'All 15 covered sectors' : scope}: ${allInScope.length} funded institutions; ${filtered.length} match the search. Allocations among matching institutions: ${eur.format(sum)}.`;
    const coverage=scope === 'all' ? '15 covered PRIN 2026 sectors' : `PRIN 2026 sector ${scope}, within the 15 covered sectors`;
    const measure=metric === 'amount' ? 'allocation to their own research units' : metric === 'led' ? 'projects led as PI' : 'projects involving their research units';
    document.getElementById('prin-institution-title').textContent=`${metric === 'amount' ? 'Own-unit MUR allocations' : metric === 'led' ? 'Projects led as PI' : 'Institutional project participation'} in ${coverage} (partial data)`;
    const matching=search.trim() ? ' matching' : '';
    const scopeNote=scope === 'all' ? '' : ` Sector ${scope}, within the 15 covered sectors.`;
    document.getElementById('prin-institution-caption').textContent=`Top ${top.length}${matching} institutions by ${measure}, fundable projects only, snapshot 7 Oct 2026.${scopeNote} Reflects partial coverage of the call and does not account for institutional size or submission volume. Not an institutional ranking.`;
  }
  function enableSorting(id,kind) {
    document.querySelectorAll(`#${id} th`).forEach(th => {
      const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.textContent=th.textContent;button.setAttribute('aria-label',`Sort by ${th.textContent}`);th.replaceChildren(button);
      button.addEventListener('click',() => {
        const key=th.dataset.key;const keyName=kind+'Key';const dirName=kind+'Dir';
        state[dirName]=state[keyName] === key ? -state[dirName] : key === 'name' || key === 'sector' ? 1 : -1;
        state[keyName]=key;kind === 'sector' ? renderSectors() : renderInstitutions();
      });
    });
  }
  data.sectors.forEach(r => {
    const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=r.sector;option.textContent=r.sector;document.getElementById('prin-institution-sector').appendChild(option);
  });
  enableSorting('prin-sectors-table','sector');enableSorting('prin-institutions-table','institution');
  document.getElementById('prin-sector-metric').addEventListener('change',renderSectors);
  ['prin-institution-sector','prin-institution-metric','prin-institution-limit'].forEach(id => document.getElementById(id).addEventListener('change',renderInstitutions));
  document.getElementById('prin-institution-search').addEventListener('input',renderInstitutions);
  renderSectors();renderInstitutions();
  document.querySelectorAll('.prin-report .prin-controls').forEach(el => { el.hidden=false; });
})();
</script>

<style>
.prin-report #prin-network-canvas { width:100%; height:680px; min-height:420px; background:#fbfaf7; border:1px solid #d5d9d5; border-radius:4px; touch-action:none; }
.prin-report .prin-network-buttons { gap:.5rem; }
.prin-report .prin-network-buttons button { padding:.55rem .75rem; background:#eaf0ec; border:1px solid #9aa6a6; border-radius:3px; min-height:42px; }
.prin-report .prin-network-buttons button:hover { background:#dce9e0; }
.prin-report .prin-network-buttons button:disabled { opacity:.5; cursor:default; }
.prin-report .prin-network-details { padding:.8rem; border-left:3px solid #23785c; background:#eaf0ec; font-size:.88rem; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
.prin-report #prin-network-fallback-image { width:100%; height:auto; }
.prin-report #prin-network-edges td:nth-child(1),.prin-report #prin-network-edges td:nth-child(2) { text-align:left; white-space:normal; min-width:200px; }
@media(max-width:600px) { .prin-report #prin-network-canvas { height:520px; } }
</style>

PISA","projects":3,"led":1,"units":3,"amount":707177.0,"partners":10,"projectCodes":["2026P3TTLE","2026PW7ZC9","2026PZNPN4"],"x":-0.04419079000135221,"y":-0.23398910063571526}],"edges":[{"source":"01279680480","target":"02772010878","shared":2,"weight":0.2222222222222222,"projects":["2026P3TTLE","2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"01279680480","target":"95039180120","shared":2,"weight":0.2222222222222222,"projects":["2026P3TTLE","2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"02772010878","target":"95039180120","shared":2,"weight":0.2222222222222222,"projects":["2026P3TTLE","2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"04400441004","target":"80007010376","shared":2,"weight":0.2222222222222222,"projects":["2026PMHZJW","2026PZNPN4"]},{"source":"80003670504","target":"80007010376","shared":2,"weight":0.2222222222222222,"projects":["2026PW7ZC9","2026PZNPN4"]},{"source":"00297640633","target":"00754150100","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PS9SLE"]},{"source":"00297640633","target":"80088230018","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PS9SLE"]},{"source":"00297640633","target":"94021400026","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PS9SLE"]},{"source":"00308780345","target":"01279680480","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P3TTLE"]},{"source":"00308780345","target":"02772010878","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P3TTLE"]},{"source":"00308780345","target":"80003670504","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P3TTLE"]},{"source":"00308780345","target":"95039180120","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P3TTLE"]},{"source":"00340520220","target":"01021630668","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PM97LY"]},{"source":"00340520220","target":"80002070524","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PM97LY"]},{"source":"00340520220","target":"80006480281","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PM97LY"]},{"source":"00340520220","target":"80088230018","shared 2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"80007010376","target":"80018670655","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PMHZJW"]},{"source":"80007010376","target":"80023730825","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PW7ZC9"]},{"source":"80007010376","target":"80054330586","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PW7ZC9"]},{"source":"80007010376","target":"95315120634","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PMHZJW"]},{"source":"80007270186","target":"80007720271","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007270186","target":"80054330586","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007270186","target":"80088230018","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007270186","target":"80209930587","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007370382","target":"80209930587","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"80007370382","target":"95039180120","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"80007720271","target":"80054330586","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007720271","target":"80088230018","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80007720271","target":"80209930587","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80018670655","target":"95315120634","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PMHZJW"]},{"source":"80023730825","target":"80054330586","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PW7ZC9"]},{"source":"80023730825","target":"82002850418","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PNB5SZ"]},{"source":"80023730825","target":"93009870234","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PNB5SZ"]},{"source":"80054330586","target":"80088230018","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80054330586","target":"80209930587","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80088230018","target":"80209930587","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026P8CJTH"]},{"source":"80088230018","target":"94021400026","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PS9SLE"]},{"source":"80209930587","target":"95039180120","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PLC5LA"]},{"source":"82002850418","target":"93009870234","shared":1,"weight":0.1111111111111111,"projects":["2026PNB5SZ"]}],"components":1,"density":0.21082621082621084,"pairOccurrences":79}]}</script>

<script src="{{ '/assets/js/cytoscape-3.33.1.min.js' | relative_url }}"></script>

<script>
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const data=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('prin-network-data').textContent);
  const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const eur=new Intl.NumberFormat('en-GB',{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0});
  let cy=null,layout=null,current=null,visibleEdges=[];
  const label=n=>n.name.replace('Alma Mater Studiorum - Università di ','').replace('Università degli Studi di ','').replace('Università degli Studi ','').replace('Consiglio Nazionale delle Ricerche','CNR');
  function setLayoutStatus(moving) {
    el('prin-network-motion').textContent=moving?'Layout moving…':'Layout settled';
    el('prin-network-stop').disabled=!moving;
  }
  function stopLayout(){if(layout)layout.stop();setLayoutStatus(false);}
  function runLayout(randomize=false) {
    if(!cy)return;
    stopLayout();
    layout=cy.layout({name:'cose',animate:!window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches,
      animationThreshold:0,refresh:20,randomize,fit:true,padding:38,componentSpacing:220,
      // Give highly connected institutions more room in the dense network core.
      nodeRepulsion:node=>65000*(1+Math.sqrt(node.degree())),
      idealEdgeLength:edge=>160+140*(1-edge.data('weight')),
      edgeElasticity:()=>160,gravity:0.08,numIter:1400,initialTemp:180,coolingFactor:0.995,minTemp:1,
      ready:()=>setLayoutStatus(true),stop:()=>setLayoutStatus(false)});
    layout.run();
  }
  function tableRows() {
    const names=new Map(current.nodes.map(n=>[n.id,n.name]));
    const body=el('prin-network-edge-body');const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();
    visibleEdges.forEach(edge=>{
      const row=document.createElement('tr');
      [names.get(edge.source),names.get(edge.target),String(edge.shared),`${edge.weight.toFixed(4)} (${(edge.weight*100).toFixed(2)}%)`].forEach(value=>{
        const cell=document.createElement('td');cell.textContent=value;row.appendChild(cell);
      });
      fragment.appendChild(row);
    });
    if(!visibleEdges.length){const row=document.createElement('tr');const cell=document.createElement('td');cell.colSpan=4;cell.textContent='No pairs reach the selected threshold.';row.appendChild(cell);fragment.appendChild(row);}
    body.replaceChildren(fragment);
  }
  function clearFocus() {
    if(cy)cy.elements().removeClass('dim focus');
    el('prin-network-details').textContent='Select an institution or a tie to see its projects and weight.';
  }
  function focusNode(node) {
    stopLayout();cy.elements().removeClass('focus').addClass('dim');node.closedNeighborhood().removeClass('dim').addClass('focus');
    const d=node.data();const neighbors=node.neighborhood().nodes().length;
    el('prin-network-details').textContent=`${d.name} · ${d.projects} fundable projects (${d.led} led as PI) · ${d.partners} partners in this full view; ${neighbors} visible at the selected threshold · Own MUR allocation ${eur.format(d.amount)}. Project codes: ${d.projectCodes.join(', ')}.`;
  }
  function focusSearch() {
    if(!cy)return;
    const query=el('prin-network-search').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
    if(!query){clearFocus();return;}
    const exact=current.nodes.find(n=>n.name.toLocaleLowerCase()===query||String(n.number)===query||n.id===query);
    const matches=current.nodes.filter(n=>n.name.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query));
    const match=exact||(matches.length===1?matches[0]:null);
    if(!match){el('prin-network-details').textContent=matches.length?`${matches.length} institutions match. Choose a full name from the suggestions.`:'No funded institution matches this search in the selected view.';return;}
    const node=cy.getElementById(match.id);focusNode(node);cy.fit(node.closedNeighborhood(),70);
  }
  function setLabels() {
    if(!cy)return;
    const mode=el('prin-network-labels').value;
    cy.nodes().forEach(node=>node.data('displayLabel',mode==='none'?'':mode==='ids'?String(node.data('number')):node.data('shortName')));
  }
  function buildView() {
    stopLayout();
    current=data.views.find(v=>v.scope===el('prin-network-scope').value);
    const coverage=current.scope === 'Overall' ? '15 covered sectors' : `sector ${current.scope}, within the 15 covered sectors`;
    el('prin-network-caption').textContent=`Co-participation in fundable PRIN 2026 projects, ${coverage} (snapshot 7 Oct 2026). A tie means two institutions share at least one funded consortium; it does not measure the amount of joint work.`;
    const minimum=Math.max(1,Math.min(current.projects,Math.floor(Number(el('prin-network-minimum').value)||1)));
    el('prin-network-minimum').value=minimum;el('prin-network-minimum').max=current.projects;
    visibleEdges=current.edges.filter(e=>e.shared>=minimum);
    el('prin-network-status').textContent=`${current.scope==='Overall'?'All 15 covered sectors':current.scope}: ${current.nodes.length} institutions, ${visibleEdges.length} of ${current.edges.length} collaboration pairs shown. Weight denominator: ${current.projects} fundable projects. The full view has ${current.components} connected component${current.components===1?'':'s'}. Filtering keeps every institution and does not change weights.`;
    const options=document.createDocumentFragment();
    current.nodes.forEach(n=>{const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=n.name;options.appendChild(option);});
    el('prin-network-institutions').replaceChildren(options);
    const graphElements=[...current.nodes.map(n=>({data:{...n,shortName:label(n),displayLabel:label(n),size:10+1.6*Math.sqrt(n.projects)},position:{x:450+n.x*350,y:330+n.y*260}})),
      ...visibleEdges.map(e=>({data:{...e,id:`${e.source}-${e.target}`,width:0.6+5*Math.sqrt(e.weight)}}))];
    if(cy){cy.destroy();cy=null;}
    el('prin-network-fallback-image').src=`{{ '/assets/img/prin-networks/' | relative_url }}${current.scope}.png`;
    el('prin-network-fallback-image').alt=`${current.scope} collaboration map: ${current.nodes.length} institutions and ${current.edges.length} ties; denominator ${current.projects} fundable projects.`;
    if(typeof window.cytoscape!=='function'){
      el('prin-network-motion').textContent='The graph could not load. The collaboration table and downloads remain available.';tableRows();return;
    }
    el('prin-network-fallback').hidden=true;el('prin-network-canvas').hidden=false;
    cy=window.cytoscape({container:el('prin-network-canvas'),elements:graphElements,layout:{name:'preset'},
      minZoom:0.08,maxZoom:6,boxSelectionEnabled:false,
      style:[
        {selector:'node',style:{'background-color':'#246080','border-color':'#fff','border-width':1,'width':'data(size)','height':'data(size)',
          'label':'data(displayLabel)','font-size':8.5,'color':'#17324a','text-wrap':'wrap','text-max-width':90,'text-valign':'bottom','text-margin-y':3,'min-zoomed-font-size':7}},
        {selector:'edge',style:{'width':'data(width)','line-color':'#23785c','opacity':0.32,'curve-style':'straight'}},
        {selector:'.dim',style:{'opacity':0.12}},
        {selector:'node.focus',style:{'border-color':'#e29b31','border-width':2.5,'z-index':10}},
        {selector:'edge.focus',style:{'opacity':0.9,'line-color':'#d9801d','z-index':9}}
      ]});
    cy.on('tap','node',event=>focusNode(event.target));
    cy.on('tap','edge',event=>{
      stopLayout();const edge=event.target,d=edge.data();cy.elements().removeClass('focus').addClass('dim');edge.connectedNodes().removeClass('dim').addClass('focus');edge.removeClass('dim').addClass('focus');
      el('prin-network-details').textContent=`${edge.source().data('name')} ↔ ${edge.target().data('name')} · ${d.shared} shared fundable project${d.shared===1?'':'s'} / ${current.projects} = ${d.weight.toFixed(4)} (${(d.weight*100).toFixed(2)}%). Project codes: ${d.projects.join(', ')}.`;
    });
    cy.on('tap',event=>{if(event.target===cy)clearFocus();});
    cy.on('grab','node',stopLayout);
    tableRows();clearFocus();setLabels();runLayout(false);
  }
  el('prin-network-scope').replaceChildren();
  data.views.forEach(view=>{const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=view.scope;option.textContent=view.scope==='Overall'?'Overall — 15 covered sectors':view.scope;el('prin-network-scope').appendChild(option);});
  el('prin-network-scope').value='Overall';
  el('prin-network-scope').addEventListener('change',()=>{el('prin-network-search').value='';buildView();});
  el('prin-network-minimum').addEventListener('change',buildView);
  el('prin-network-labels').addEventListener('change',setLabels);
  el('prin-network-focus').addEventListener('click',focusSearch);
  el('prin-network-search').addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter')focusSearch();});
  el('prin-network-reset').addEventListener('click',()=>{el('prin-network-search').value='';clearFocus();if(cy)cy.fit(undefined,38);});
  el('prin-network-relayout').addEventListener('click',()=>runLayout(true));
  el('prin-network-stop').addEventListener('click',stopLayout);
  el('prin-network-zoom-in').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(cy)cy.zoom({level:Math.min(cy.maxZoom(),cy.zoom()*1.25),renderedPosition:{x:cy.width()/2,y:cy.height()/2}});});
  el('prin-network-zoom-out').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(cy)cy.zoom({level:Math.max(cy.minZoom(),cy.zoom()/1.25),renderedPosition:{x:cy.width()/2,y:cy.height()/2}});});
  el('prin-network-png').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(!cy)return;stopLayout();
    const scope=current.scope, minimum=el('prin-network-minimum').value;
    const image=new Image();
    image.onload=()=>{
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width=Math.max(image.width,960);canvas.height=image.height+112;
      const context=canvas.getContext('2d');
      context.fillStyle='#fbfaf7';context.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      context.drawImage(image,(canvas.width-image.width)/2,0);
      context.fillStyle='#17324a';context.textAlign='center';context.font='bold 24px sans-serif';
      context.fillText('15 sectors, partial data, 7 Oct 2026',canvas.width/2,image.height+34,canvas.width-32);
      context.font='18px sans-serif';
      context.fillText(`${scope} co-participation in fundable projects; minimum ${minimum} shared project(s).`,canvas.width/2,image.height+65,canvas.width-32);
      context.fillText('Ties indicate a shared consortium, not the amount of joint work.',canvas.width/2,image.height+94,canvas.width-32);
      const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      link.download=`PRIN-2026-${scope}-collaboration.png`;link.click();
    };
    image.src=cy.png({full:true,scale:2,bg:'#fbfaf7'});
  });
  el('prin-network-current-json').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(!current)return;const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({...current,minimumSharedProjects:Number(el('prin-network-minimum').value),displayedEdges:visibleEdges},null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download=`PRIN-2026-${current.scope}-collaboration.json`;link.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(cy)cy.resize();});
  buildView();
  document.querySelectorAll('#prin-network-panel .prin-controls').forEach(control=>{control.hidden=false;});
})();

</script>
